So it is not a load artefact and not specific to any one machine.\n\nIt survives every timeout vitest offers: --testTimeout=15000 --hookTimeout=15000 --teardownTimeout=10000 still exits 124. Those abort a slow test BODY, so what survives them is a worker that will not exit -- an open handle (timer, socket, or worker thread) keeping the event loop alive after tests finish. Consistent with environment: jsdom plus the OPFS mock in test/setup.ts, though the specific handle was not isolated.\n\nIt also leaks: a vitest worker reached 1.9 GB RSS at 13 seconds old, with another node process at 2.6 GB. It compounds because bf-25xr and bf-2dba are both ABOUT the test suite, so any worker claiming one runs npm test, and a worker unit with Restart=always retries forever.\n\nNote bf-25xr is mismeasured: a suite that hangs never reports 225 results, so \"41 of 225 failing\" came from a run that completed.
